	#Blair's premiership
	focus = {
		id = ENG_blair_prem
		icon = ENG_blair_start

		x = 20
		y = 1
		cost = 2

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

		available = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Tony Blair"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus ENG_blair_prem"
			add_political_power = 75
			add_stability = 0.03
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { ruling_party = 3 } } }
					set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { enable_elections = 0 }
					change_ruling_party_effect = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = {
								name = "Tony Blair"
								ruling_only = yes
							}
						}
					}
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Tony Blair"
						picture = "tony_blair.dds"
						ideology = socialism
						traits = {
							western_socialism
							career_politician
							judiciary
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 50
		}
	}

	#Starmageddon
	focus = {
		id = ENG_starmageddon
		icon = ENG_starmer_start

		x = 27
		y = 18
		cost = 2

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

		available = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Keir Starmer"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus ENG_starmageddon"
			add_political_power = 75
			add_stability = 0.03
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { ruling_party = 3 } } }
					set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { enable_elections = 0 }
					change_ruling_party_effect = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = {
								name = "Keir Starmer"
								ruling_only = yes
							}
						}
					}
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Keir Starmer"
						picture = "keir_starmer.dds"
						ideology = socialism
						traits = {
							western_socialism
							judiciary
						}
					}
				}

			country_event = britain_may.1
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { ruling_party = 1 } } }
					set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { enable_elections = 0 }
					change_ruling_party_effect = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = {
								name = "Theresa May"
								ruling_only = yes
							}
						}
					}
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Theresa May"
						picture = "Theresa_May.dds"
						ideology = conservatism
						traits = {
							western_conservatism
							economic_managers
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

			base = 100 
		}

	### Brexit ###

	#Destination: Brexit
	focus = {
		id = ENG_destination_brexit
		icon = ENG_brexit

		x = 73
		y = 8
		cost = 2

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY }

		available = {
			has_idea = EU_member
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = eng_euroscept_tt
				check_variable = { ROOT.eurosceptic > 0.50 }
			}	
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus ENG_destination_brexit"
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
			change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
			set_temp_variable = { modify_eurosceptic = 0.25 }
			EU_eurosceptic_change = yes
			add_ideas = ENG_split_brexit
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "David Cameron"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = britain_brexit.2
			}
			news_event = britain_brexit.1
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				date > 2016.01.01
				add = 1000
			}
			modifier = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "David Cameron"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
				add = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Michael Howard"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
				add = -970
			}
		}
	}
